Once past interactions are stored as soft slots, resizing a record changes the input seen by a frozen LLM. Capacity allocation, re-encoding, and readout are therefore coupled, and a fixed-budget compressor is not enough.","importance":"The work is not primarily about foundation-model pretraining, but it is useful for thinking about long-context memory interfaces. Its main idea is to start from verifiable hard-prompt embeddings and then soften them gradually through learned width adaptation. This is relevant to external memory, soft prompt caches, and agent state systems that must work with a frozen base model.","technical_approach":"HasMem keeps a Soft Bank and a recurrent Global state outside the frozen backbone. Each memory entry starts from the frozen model’s token embeddings. A Controller chooses KEEP, SHRINK, or EXPAND for each record. When width changes, a Writer re-encodes the slot using pooling or interpolation plus a Global-conditioned residual. A Reader adds low-rank readout adaptation to selected attention output projections, allowing the frozen model to better consume softened memory. A causal gate preserves equivalence to the hard prompt when all actions are KEEP and retrieval/input construction are identical. Training uses downstream QA supervision together with a penalty on cumulative memory positions, so the model learns a quality-budget tradeoff.","experiments":"The main setup uses a frozen Qwen2.5-7B backbone with k=8. Expert pruning reduces this storage burden; at a fixed pruning budget, the goal is to preserve the original model's output distribution as closely as possible. We introduce RAZOR, a training-free expert pruning method ","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-24T19:02:55Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_30465.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"RAZOR 解决的是 MoE 大模型的整专家剪枝问题：在不做恢复训练的条件下，给定每层保留专家数，如何删掉对原模型输出分布破坏最小的专家。论文的关键判断是，专家是否常被路由、输出范数是否大，并不能直接说明它是否重要。真正决定删除损伤的是它的功能能否被剩余专家替代，尤其要考虑删除后幸存专家权重重归一化，以及 router refill 可能把原本未选中的专家补进来。","importance":"这篇工作对 MoE 基座模型压缩有直接参考价值。它不是提出新的 MoE 训练方法，而是给出一个训练后、无梯度、无恢复训练的专家裁剪准则。贡献主要在于把专家重要性从“单个专家统计量”转向“相对原始 mixture 输出的几何可替代性”。这对大规模 MoE 的部署和持续预训练前模型瘦身都实用，也提醒预训练研究者：MoE 专家的功能冗余不能只看路由频率或激活强度，必须看专家输出在向量空间中的组合关系。论文也比较克制地指出，局部输出保持不等于生成行为稳定，任务分数、预测分布 KL、自由生成退化需要分开评估。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 consensus residual。对一个 token 的某层 MoE，原始输出是被 router 选中的 k 个专家的加权和，论文称这个加权和为 consensus。这里的 consensus 不是假设专家意见一致，而是要保存的原始混合输出。每个专家的 residual 是它相对该 consensus 的偏差。删除某个已选专家时，剩余专家的权重会重新归一化；如果启用 refill，还会由 router 提升一个原本未进入 top-k 的专家。RAZOR 推导了固定层输入下单个专家删除造成输出变化的精确恒等式，把 survivor renormalization 和 router-selected refill 同时纳入评分。无 refill 的情况是特例，删除专家 j 的输出变化可以写成与 f_j 和原 consensus 的差有关的项，而不是只依赖 w_j 或 ||f_j||。算法在校准 token 上前向运行，逐层累计每个专家的局部删除损伤，然后在每层预算内保留损伤大的专家、剪掉更容易被替代的专家。它不做子集搜索，不用梯度，也不做 pruning 后 recovery training。与基于路由频率、贡献范数、加权范数的方法相比，RAZOR 保留了专家输出方向信息；与只看 router transition 的方法相比，它直接度量 MoE 输出层面的几何变化。","experiments":"实验覆盖 GLM-4.7-Flash、Qwen3.6-35B-A3B、DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731 和 Hy3 四个 MoE backbone，在 25% 和 50% 整专家删除预算下评估。RAZOR 在全部 8 个模型-预算设置中取得最高的九任务 macro average。对于同时报告 REAP 的两个 backbone，RAZOR 比 REAP 高 2.12 到 5.59 个 macro 分数点。论文还在匹配的 GLM-4.7-Flash 和 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B checkpoint 上计算 reverse KL，RAZOR 在 4 个模型-预算组合中都低于 REAP，说明它不只是提高任务分数，也更接近原模型预测分布。消融层面的主要信息是：仅看使用频率、输出范数或加权输出范数会遗漏专家输出方向；考虑 refill 会改变最优删除选择；aggressive pruning 仍然有明显损失，50% 删除时四个 backbone 相比原模型均有 macro 性能下降。自由生成分析显示，任务保持和生成稳定不是同一个指标。以 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 不足以决定最小损伤删除。Methodology 是核心，重点看 consensus residual 的定义、无 refill 删除恒等式、带 refill 的扩展，以及如何在 calibration tokens 上聚合逐专家分数。实验部分建议看四个 backbone 在 25%/50% 剪枝下的九任务 macro average、reverse KL 对比、以及自由生成分析。最值得深挖的是局部 surrogate 与全局行为之间的缝隙：单层固定输入的欧氏输出变化为什么能在任务上有效，但为什么仍不能保证多层传播后的生成稳定。这一点对后续做 MoE pruning、expert merging、continual pretrain 前的结构瘦身都很关键。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"RAZOR targets whole-expert pruning for MoE LLMs. Given a per-layer expert budget and no recovery training, the goal is to remove experts while preserving the original model behavior as much as possible. The paper argues that usage frequency, gate mass, or output norm are incomplete pruning signals. The relevant quantity is functional replaceability: whether the remaining routed computation can reproduce the contribution of the removed expert after weight renormalization and possible router refill.","importance":"The contribution is directly useful for MoE model compression and deployment. It reframes expert pruning from isolated expert importance to the geometry of the original weighted mixture. For pretraining researchers, the main lesson is that MoE redundancy is not well described by routing statistics alone. Two experts with similar usage and norm can have very different deletion damage if their output directions play different roles in the mixture. The work also separates three outcomes that are often conflated: downstream task retention, predictive fidelity measured by KL, and free-generation stability.","technical_approach":"$15","experiments":"The evaluation uses four MoE backbones: GLM-4.7-Flash, Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731, and Hy3. Each is tested at 25% and 50% expert removal. RAZOR achieves the best nine-task macro average in all eight model-budget settings. On the two backbones where REAP is also benchmarked, RAZOR improves the macro average by 2.12 to 5.59 points. On matched GLM-4.7-Flash and Qwen3.6-35B-A3B checkpoints, RAZOR also obtains lower reverse KL than REAP in all four model-budget settings, indicating better preservation of the original predictive distribution. The paper is careful about the limits: 50% expert removal still degrades all four backbones relative to the original models. In the experiments, prioritize the nine-task macro tables, reverse-KL comparisons, and free-generation diagnostics. For Llama-3-70B in BF16, the 140 GB weight footprint exceeds the 80 GB HBM of a single accelerator, and one 128k-token sequence adds 42 GB of KV cache. Techniques that compress, evict, page, share, or offload KV state have proliferated, but reported gains use inconsistent workloads, hardwar","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-25T06:01:27Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_30854.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.DC","cs.LG","cs.PF"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是长上下文自回归推理中的真实瓶颈定位问题：decode 阶段不是主要受算力限制，而是受 HBM 带宽限制；随着上下文长度增长，限制资源会从模型权重读取转向 KV cache 读取。已有 KV 压缩、淘汰、分页、共享、卸载方法很多，但实验设置、硬件、上下文长度、batch size、质量指标互不一致，导致结果难以比较，也难以判断哪类方法适合哪种服务场景。","importance":"这篇 SoK 对基座预训练研究也有直接价值。长上下文能力不是只靠训练出更长 RoPE 或更强 retrieval pattern 就能落地，推理时 KV cache 的线性增长会反过来约束可训练的上下文长度、注意力结构、GQA/MQA/MLA 设计、上下文压缩目标和 serving-aware architecture。论文把 KV cache 从工程细节提升为模型设计约束：每 token KV 字节数、attention 读带宽、batching 能否摊薄权重读取、KV 是否能共享，都会影响长上下文模型的有效成本。对做 pretrain 的人，最有用的是它给出了一套可计算的硬件-模型-上下文框架，用来评估架构改动是否真的缓解了内存墙，而不是只看 benchmark 分数。","technical_approach":"论文的核心是用 roofline/bytes-per-token 分析统一 KV cache 相关技术。它先分别计算 decode 阶段的权重读取和 KV attention 读取。batch size 为 1 时，参数量为 P、权重宽度为 wp 的前向大约有 2P FLOPs、读取 P·wp 字节，BF16 下 arithmetic intensity 约为 1 FLOP/B；H100 SXM 的 BF16 dense ridge point 约为 295 FLOP/B，因此单请求 decode 远在带宽受限区域。KV attention 同样如此：单个 query attend 到 s 个 cache token，每个 head 约 4sdh FLOPs，读取 2sdh·wkv 字节，BF16 下约 1 FLOP/B；GQA 以 group factor g 提高复用，g=8 也只有约 8 FLOP/B，仍远低于 H100 ridge point。论文进一步指出 batching 只能摊薄权重读取，不能摊薄 KV 读取，因为每个 sequence 有自己的 KV cache；所以当上下文足够长时，KV traffic 会超过 weight traffic，batching 的吞吐杠杆会变弱。随后，论文把已有方法按机制分为五类：KV quantization 降低每元素宽度；token eviction 删除低价值 token，常见策略保留 attention sink 和 heavy hitter；KV paging 用固定块管理 cache，减少碎片并支持共享；prefix caching 复用共享 prompt 前缀；heterogeneous memory tiering 在 HBM、host DRAM、NVMe 间迁移 KV，用互连带宽换 HBM 容量。它强调这些机制原则上可组合，但必须在同一 workload、硬件和质量口径下评估。","experiments":"论文给出的关键数字很有用。Llama-3-70B 在 BF16 下权重约 140 GB，已经超过单张 80 GB HBM 加速卡容量。其 KV cache 公式为 Bkv = 2·L·nkv·dh·wkv·s。对 Llama-3-70B，L=80、nkv=8、dh=128、BF16 每元素 2 字节，因此每 token KV cache 为 327,680 字节。单条 128k-token 序列会额外产生约 42 GB KV state；到 10^6 tokens 时约为 328 GB，相当于超过四张 H100 80GB 的总 HBM。算力利用侧，BF16 decode 的权重读取 intensity 约 1 FLOP/B，而 H100 ridge point 约 295 FLOP/B；GQA 即使 group factor 为 8，attention-side intensity 也只有约 8 FLOP/B。batching 的分界也被明确量化：权重侧 intensity 随 batch size 线性上升，H100 BF16 下接近 ridge 需要 batch size 约 295；KV 侧 intensity 不随 batch size 改善。论文的实验价值不在提出新 kernel，而在把各类 reported gains 拆成 derived claims 和 measured claims，并要求在一致协议下比较吞吐、延迟、显存、上下文长度和质量损失。","reading_guide":"建议精读。先读 Introduction 和 Decode-Phase Memory Wall，重点看 arithmetic intensity 的推导，不要只记结论。这里解释了为什么长上下文 decode 的瓶颈不是普通意义上的 attention FLOPs，而是 KV bytes。然后精读 Eq. (1) 和 Figure 1，对照 Llama-3 family 的 KV per token 曲线，手算自己模型的 KV 容量：L、nkv、dh、wkv、context length 哪个更敏感。第三个重点是 weight traffic 与 KV traffic 的 crossover 推导，它直接影响 serving batch size、prefill/decode 分离和多请求调度。最后读五类技术的 taxonomy 和 measurement protocol，尤其关注质量指标是否可比：KV quantization 的困点通常在低 bit value 精度和长程召回；eviction 的困点在未来注意力不可知；paging 解决碎片和管理，不减少理论 KV 字节；prefix caching 依赖 workload 共享前缀；offload/tiering 受 PCIe/NVLink/DRAM/NVMe 带宽限制。值得深挖的是：MLA、GQA、sliding-window attention、state-space/hybrid architecture、latent memory compression 能否在这个框架下改变线性 KV law，而不只是改变常数。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper identifies the binding resource in long-context autoregressive decoding. Decode is not primarily limited by arithmetic throughput; it is limited by memory bandwidth. As sequence length grows, the dominant traffic shifts from model weights to the KV cache. The literature has many KV compression, eviction, paging, sharing, and offloading techniques, but their reported gains are hard to compare because workloads, hardware targets, context lengths, batching regimes, and quality metrics differ.","importance":"The paper is useful for pretraining researchers because it turns KV cache cost into a model-design constraint. Long-context pretraining cannot be evaluated only by loss curves or long-context benchmarks; the deployed model must also pay a linear KV cost per generated token. The analysis directly informs choices such as GQA versus MQA versus MLA, attention head layout, long-context extension, cache-aware architecture search, and whether a proposed memory mechanism changes the scaling law or only its constant factor. The main contribution is not a new serving trick. It is a quantitative framework that connects architecture, hardware bandwidth, context length, and serving workload.","technical_approach":"$16","experiments":"$17","reading_guide":"Read it closely if you work on long-context models, attention variants, or inference-aware pretraining. Then study the KV-cache size equation and reproduce the Llama-3-70B calculation for your own model. The next key part is the crossover between weight traffic and KV traffic, because it determines when batching stops being the main throughput lever. The taxonomy of techniques should be read as a mechanism map rather than a list of systems papers: quantization changes bytes per element, eviction changes retained length, paging changes allocation efficiency, prefix caching exploits request overlap, and tiering trades HBM pressure for interconnect or storage bandwidth. We identify two distinct failure modes of standard post-training quantization. On Huginn-3.5B, per-channel INT4 fails primarily at the non-residual loop-entry adapter, while quantizing the residual core is much less damaging. We call this feedback exposure: a quantized layer perturbs the recurrent state without an identity path, and the resulting error is fed back at later steps. Control","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-25T04:57:33Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_30820.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文要解决的是：looped transformer 这类复用同一组权重进行多步递归的模型，为什么常规低比特 PTQ 会失败，以及失败是否来自普通量化误差之外的递归结构因素。","importance":"这篇工作对基座研究有直接价值。looped transformer、latent recurrence、recurrent reasoning 模型都试图用较少参数换更深计算。低比特量化看似更适合这类模型，因为共享权重的压缩收益更高。但论文说明，递归复用会改变量化误差的风险结构：某些层的误差会被反馈到后续状态里，常规单步校准也可能看不到后续递归使用的激活方向。这个结论对训练架构、量化部署、以及递归式推理模型的稳定性分析都有参考意义。","technical_approach":"论文区分了两个独立问题。第一个是 feedback exposure：如果某个被量化层位于递归状态入口，而且没有 residual identity path，量化扰动会直接进入 recurrent state，并在后续递归中反复被读取和放大。Huginn-3.5B 和 R-Llama 的 loop-entry adapter 属于这类位置；相比之下，residual core 的量化误差被 identity path 缓冲，损害小得多。作者用局部 Jacobian 的 spectral radius 作为稳定性诊断，分析递归状态是否存在局部放大方向。第二个问题是 calibration blindness：GPTQ 的一阶校准通常只使用 shared layer 第 0 次调用的激活来构造 Hessian 近似 H=X^T X，但 looped model 的同一组权重会在后续递归步处理不同的状态分布。若后续步使用了 step-0 激活里没有覆盖的输入方向，GPTQ 会在错误子空间上优化量化误差。作者因此比较了 step-0 calibration 与 trajectory calibration，即在所有 N 次 invocation 上累计激活来构造校准矩阵。方法上还区分 per-channel INT4 和 grouped INT4，后者以 group size 128 减小量化误差但增加存储开销。","experiments":"关键结果比较清楚。Huginn-3.5B 在 100 个 GSM8K 样本上，bf16 exact match 为 34%；对 loop-entry adapter 和 recurrent core 做 per-channel INT4 后降到 0%。进一步定位显示，在 50 个 prompt 的 token prediction 后的 44.8% 随 8 次 latent recurrence 上升到 61.5%，说明 residual 结构下误差不一定随递归恶化。常规敏感性指标没有解释这一点：小随机输入扰动把 adapter 排在 17 个 projection 中最不敏感，Hessian trace 也把一个 core projection 排成高敏感，但单独量化 adapter 的实际损害更大。对 grouped INT4，立即崩溃减少，但 one-step GPTQ 在 9 个 checkpoint 中有 5 个不如 round-to-nearest。增加 calibration prompt 数量不能解决该问题，因为缺的是后续递归轨迹中的方向，而不是样本数。线性滤波器和 Mamba 控制实验表明 feedback exposure 不限于 Downstream computation may be predictable, or may have run before, yet it cannot begin until the model or the user resolves the branch. We call this serialization the branch-resolution barrier. Caching alone does not hide it: the key that identifies a reusable result is not known until then. In this paper, we propose DynBranch, which makes an unresolved branch addressable before it resolves. Its stable coordinate lets candidate subgr","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-25T09:42:18Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_31047.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.DC","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 agentic LLM serving 里的一个具体但常被忽略的瓶颈：分支尚未解析时，下游子图既不能启动，也无法命中可复用结果，导致大量本可并行或复用的计算被串行化。作者把这个问题定义为 branch-resolution barrier。难点不只是“提前猜分支”这么简单，而是三件事要同时成立：一，复用单位不是整条请求，而是请求内部反复出现的 subgraph；二，复用键在分支真正落定前并不存在，但又希望在这段等待窗口里先把候选工作做起来；三，提前执行和复用都要和在线 serving 负载联动，否则低负载下有益的 speculation 到高负载时会反过来拖慢系统。","importance":"对基座预训练本身的直接贡献不大，但对大模型系统研究，尤其是 agent-native serving，有较清楚的方法价值。它把“缓存”和“推测执行”从两个分离机制，统一到一个 branch-state 抽象里，重点不是模型算法，而是把 unresolved branch 变成可寻址对象。这个思路对未来多步推理、tool-use、multi-call inference 的系统设计有启发：随着模型从单轮 completion 走向长链路 agent，延迟瓶颈会越来越多地来自分支后的串行子图，而不是单次 decode。本工作给出的贡献量级主要体现在 serving 层：在不改 agent harness、不改底层推理引擎的前提下，拿到可观延迟下降，并且保持结果等价。","technical_approach":"方法核心有三层。第一层是可复用对象的定义。DynBranch 不再把缓存边界放在整请求或 prefix token 上，而是记录“已完成的 branch subgraph + 触发它的输入 + 它读过的外部依赖”。后续请求只有在输入精确匹配、且这些外部 reads 仍然新鲜时，才能消费该结果；一旦相关外部状态发生写入，就失效。这解决了 agent 工作流里 retrieval/tool 结果依赖外部环境、不能只靠 prompt exact match 复用的问题。\n\n第二层是 unresolved branch 的可寻址化。作者利用 workflow template 中在分支解析前就已知的 stable coordinate，也就是某个决策点在模板中的身份，例如 router stage、某个固定工具选择位点。借助这个坐标，系统可以在分支尚未真正解析时，先为若干候选分支启动 fills。这里的 fill 可以理解为“在 resolution window 内预跑的候选子图执行”。关键约束是：这些 speculative fills 在分支真正选定前对外不可见，只有当后续 canonical demand 到来，并且 exact match 与 freshness 都满足时，才会被提升为可用结果。因此错误预测只会浪费资源，不会改变语义结果。\n\n第三层是 admission control。作者设计了两级在线控制器：一级决定要不要生成候选分支，二级决定每个候选 fill 是否执行。控制依据是 expected benefit 是否超过 load price。这个 price 不是静态阈值，而是读取当前引擎 decode 占用和 KV 占用，按 fill 的执行时间窗动态定价。这样在低负载时更积极地做 speculation，在高负载时自动收缩，避免 speculative work 抢占 canonical 请求资源。文中还提到控制器从延迟反馈中在线学习收益估计，而不是只靠固定启发式。\n\n系统实现上，DynBranch 部署在 model-API boundary，位于 agent harness 与 SGLang/vLLM 之间，因此不要求修改底层模型执行引擎，也不要求 agent 框架配合改写。这一点决定了它更像一个 serving middleware，而不是新的 agent runtime。","experiments":"实验设置以 Qwen3-32B、4×H200 为主，也补充了 Qwen3-8B/RTX 4090 的 commodity 环境。作者声称在四类 agent workload 和生产 trace 上评估了 branch-resolution barrier 的占比，并指出在测得的 coding、research、scientific agents 中，45%–57% 的端到端延迟来自模型输出之后被串行化的下游时间，这给方法提供了问题规模上的依据。\n\n效果上，DynBranch 相比各 workload 上最强的既有系统，在同样 GPU 条件下平均延迟最高下降 32%；相对 no-reuse floor，下降 46%–66%。如果关闭 reuse，只保留 early fill，模型驱动路径上的平均延迟仍可下降 8.5%–34.4%；在人机交互场景里，user-turn latency 下降 46.6%–47.7%。这说明收益并不完全来自跨请求复用，单纯利用 resolution window 做 speculative overlap 也有明显作用。\n\n从论文给出的结构看，值得注意的实验点还有三类。第一类是 correctness：作者强调 workflow results preserved，说明 speculative fills 只在 canonical authorization 后可见，语义上保持与基线一致。第二类是负载敏感性：两级控制器应当在不同负载下展示不同 admission 行为，否则很难解释为什么 speculation 不会在高负载时伤害 tail latency。第三类是泛化性：作者提到收益在不同 backbone family 上持续存在，说明方法依赖的是 workflow 结构和 serving 时序，而不是某个模型特性。\n\n由于给出的全文片段没有完整表格，无法可靠补充更多 workload 级别的绝对延迟、命中率、控制器 ablation 数字；但从摘要和引言可确认，核心结论是“reuse + early fill + load-aware admission”三者组合优于单独缓存或单独 speculation。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，重点不是模型细节，而是系统抽象是否站得住。建议先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1/2，确认作者定义的 branch-resolution barrier 是否覆盖你熟悉的 agent workload。然后看 3.2–3.4：这里是方法真正有信息量的部分，分别对应 subgraph reuse 的正确性边界、branch-state abstraction、两级控制器。阅读时要特别留意两个问题：一，stable coordinate 在多大范围内成立，是否依赖 workflow template 足够固定；二，external reads 分钟可以抓住主要内容。如果只想判断是否值得进一步跟进，先看问题定义、branch-state 设计和主表即可。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific systems bottleneck in agentic LLM serving: before a branch is resolved, downstream subgraphs cannot start and previously reusable results cannot be addressed, so a large fraction of potentially parallel or reusable work is serialized. The authors call this the branch-resolution barrier. The challenge is not just branch prediction. Three constraints must hold together: the reusable unit is an internal subgraph rather than a whole request; the key that authorizes reuse does not exist until the branch resolves, yet work should begin during that waiting window; and speculative execution must be priced against online serving load, otherwise a strategy that helps at low load can hurt under contention.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is a meaningful serving paper for agent-native LLM systems. Its main contribution is to unify caching and speculation under a single branch-state abstraction, with the key idea of making unresolved branches addressable. That matters because as LLM usage shifts from single-turn completion to multi-step tool-using agents, latency increasingly comes from serialized post-decision subgraphs rather than a single decode pass. The contribution is mainly at the serving layer: noticeable latency reduction without modifying agent harnesses or the underlying inference engine, while preserving exact workflow outputs.","technical_approach":"$1a","experiments":"$1b","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. The value is in the systems abstraction, not in model internals. Start with the Introduction and Figures 1–2 to see whether the branch-resolution barrier matches the agent workloads you care about. Then read Sections 3.2–3.4 carefully. Those sections contain the real substance: correctness boundaries for subgraph reuse, the branch-state abstraction, and the two-level controller. Two questions are worth keeping in mind while reading: how broadly the stable-coordinate assumption holds, and how expensive dependency tracking and invalidation become in realistic tool ecosystems.\n\nIn the experiments, prioritize Sections 5.4–5.6. Section 5.4 should isolate the gains from early fill and the controller, 5.5 should show load-aware admission behavior, and 5.6 should justify the assumption that decision points are template-identifiable before resolution. $\\textit{mentored","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-24T19:12:41Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_30474.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作研究有损 f-divergence。作者先分析 total variation 距离，指出最优解集合有清晰的几何结构：它是由 drafter 与 target 坐标形成的高维超矩形、概率单纯形以及激活的散度约束的交集。这个集合不仅大，而且包含所有严格凸 f-divergence 的最优解。随后推广到任意 f-divergence。对严格凸生成函数，最优 mentored 分布唯一，并具有逐坐标 clamping 形式：π* = max{αq, min{p, βq}}，其中 q 是 target，p 是 drafter，0 ≤ α < 1 < β。这个形式的意义在于，每个词的概率都被限制在 target 概率的上下倍数之间；它沿着一条从 target 到 drafter 的一维轨迹移动，而且轨迹与具体 f-divergence 无关。作者还证明，在 speculative decoding 证明，除非需要把该框架改造成模型选择准则。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies why lossy speculative decoding can increase decoding speed and, in some cases, improve output quality relative to the target model. Exact speculative decoding must preserve the target distribution, so its acceptance rate is tightly capped by the mismatch between the drafter and the target. Mentored decoding relaxes this constraint: the output distribution may deviate from the target within a bounded divergence budget. The central question is how to characterize the optimal distribution under this constraint and when the resulting model combination can be better than the target alone.","importance":"The value for foundation-model research is mainly conceptual and algorithmic. The paper treats lossy decoding as a distributional optimization problem rather than a heuristic acceptance rule. It clarifies how much acceptance-rate gain can be bought with a controlled distributional drift, and it gives a route to reason about quality gains through a boosting lens. A practical implication is that the drafter should not be viewed only as a cheap approximation to the target. It can be a complementary model, and drafter selection should consider complementarity in addition to acceptance probability or KL proximity.","technical_approach":"$1c","experiments":"The provided content indicates a theory-heavy paper rather than an empirical benchmarking paper. Quantization errors persist according to channel decay rates. We derive distortion weights from the observability Gramian and combine them with normalized state ranges for mixed-precision bit allocation, without calibration data, rotation, or training. We also quantize decay rates logarithmically. With per-token state quantization, a four-bit mean payload reduces excess negative","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-25T08:01:56Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_30950.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究 hybrid language model 中固定大小 recurrent state 的低比特量化问题。不同于 KV cache，recurrent state 的量化误差会在后续 token 中持续传播；衰减慢的通道会保留误差更久，衰减快的通道会更快消散。因此，简单按当前范围做均匀量化会把 bit 分给不该优先保护的通道。","importance":"对预训练和架构研究的价值在于，它把 hybrid LM 的推理内存问题和模型动力学联系起来。Kimi Delta Attention、Gated DeltaNet、Mamba-2 这类模型用固定 recurrent state 替代或补充注意力上下文，服务端瓶颈从 KV cache 转向 state 读写带宽和精度。论文给出一个无需校准数据、无需旋转、无需再训练的 bit allocation 规则，说明 recurrent state 压缩不能只照搬 KV-cache 量化。对设计长上下文或线性/混合架构的预训练模型来说，这类分析能反过来影响 state 维度、decay 分布、normalization group 和量化友好性的选择。","technical_approach":"核心方法是用 observability Gramian 给每个 state 通道分配失真权重。直观地说，同样大小的量化噪声，如果落在慢衰减且后续 query 经常读取的通道上，会产生更大的累计 readout error。在线性噪声近似下，稳态 readout MSE 可写成 sum_i Gamma_i sigma_i^2，其中 Gamma_i 由衰减、query 能量和写回间隔决定。对于对角衰减且无 erasure 的情形，Gamma_i = rho_i / (C expm1(2 s_i))；s_i 越小，通道记忆越长，权重越大。论文再把 Gamma_i 与按 normalization group 归一化后的 state range 结合，用高率量化理论做 mixed-precision bit allocation。decay gate 本身也用 log s 上均匀的量化，因为小 s 区域对有效时间常数更敏感。方法不依赖离线校准集，这一点和很多 activation/KV 量化方法不同。","experiments":"实验覆盖三个 hybrid model，并比较七类 baseline。按 token 对 recurrent state 做量化时，平均 4-bit payload 相比最佳 baseline 将 excess negative log-likelihood 降低 3.3 到 27.9 倍；到 6 bits 时，NLL 与 quantization。文中还给出局限：确定性 rounding 在 C=1 的逐 token 写回下会产生相关误差，模拟中 lag-one error correlation 最高到 0.23；三到五比特时 readout error 可为独立噪声假设的 0.17 到 2.4 倍，六比特后偏差通常收敛到 20% 以内。因此 Gramian 权重更适合作为实用启发式，而不是精确误差预测器。","reading_guide":"泛读建议用 25 到 35 分钟。重点看 Section 2 的误差传播推导，尤其是 Gamma_i 的来源和 C 对失真的作用；再看 Section 3 的 bit allocation 与 log decay quantization。实验部分建议优先看 4-bit 和 6-bit NLL 表、metadata 讨论、以及消融表。值得留意的细节是：论文没有把 recurrent state 当成普通 activation 来压，而是按“误差未来会被读出多少次”来分配比特。这一点对 hybrid LM serving 和后续预训练架构设计都有参考意义。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies low-bit quantization of recurrent states in hybrid language models. Unlike ordinary activations or KV entries, errors in recurrent states persist and affect future readouts. Channels with slow decay retain quantization noise for many steps, while fast-decaying channels dissipate it quickly. Uniform range-based quantization therefore allocates precision without considering the future impact of the error.","importance":"The main value for pretraining and architecture work is the connection between hybrid-LM state dynamics and inference-time memory efficiency. Models such as Kimi Delta Attention, Gated DeltaNet, and Mamba-2 replace part of the attention cache with fixed-size recurrent states, shifting the systems bottleneck toward state storage, read/write bandwidth, and numerical precision. The paper provides a data-free, training-free, rotation-free allocation rule for recurrent-state quantization. It also makes a broader point: KV-cache quantization heuristics are not directly sufficient for recurrent architectures, because state errors have temporal dynamics. This can inform future choices of decay parameterization, state grouping, normalization, and quantization-friendly architecture design.","technical_approach":"The method assigns a distortion weight to each state channel using an observability Gramian. Under a local linear noise model, steady-state readout MSE can be approximated as sum_i Gamma_i sigma_i^2, where sigma_i^2 is the quantization noise variance for a row and Gamma_i measures how strongly that row’s error will be observed in future outputs. For diagonal decay without erasure, the paper derives Gamma_i = rho_i / (C expm1(2 s_i)), where s_i is the decay rate, rho_i is query-weighted energy, and C is the write-back interval. Slow decay therefore receives higher precision. These weights are combined with normalized state ranges inside output-normalization groups and plugged into a classical high-rate mixed-precision bit-allocation rule. Decay rates are quantized uniformly in log s, reflecting the sensitivity of long time constants to small relative changes in decay.","experiments":"The experiments evaluate three hybrid models and seven baselines. With per-token recurrent-state quantization, a 4-bit mean payload reduces excess negative log-likelihood by factors of 3.3 to 27.9 relative to the best baseline. At 6 bits, the NLL gap to the FP32-state baseline is below 0.005 nats. The paper includes ablations separating the contributions of variable bit widths, decay-aware weighting, range normalization, and logarithmic decay-rate quantization. It also reports an important caveat: deterministic rounding at C=1 can create correlated errors. In simulations using Kimi-like decays and rank-one writes, lag-one error correlations reach 0.23 for very slow channels, and three-to-five-bit readout error can range from 0.17x to 2.4x the independent-noise prediction. By six bits, discrepancies are typically within 20%. The Gramian rule is therefore best read as a robust allocation heuristic, not as an exact error model.","reading_guide":"A useful skim should take about 25–35 minutes. Start with the error-propagation derivation, especially the definition of Gamma_i and the role of the write-back interval C. Then read the mixed-precision allocation rule and the log-space decay quantization section. In the experiments, focus on the 4-bit and 6-bit NLL comparisons, metadata overhead, and the ablation table. The key idea to retain is that recurrent-state quantization should allocate bits according to future observability of the error, not only according to instantaneous range.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"如果你在看 hybrid/SSM-LM 的部署，这篇比常见 PTQ 更有味道；先看 observability 权重的推导和 4-bit NLL 表，别把它误当通用 Transformer 结论。","en":"Worth a skim if you care about hybrid or stateful LMs in deployment; focus on the observability-based weighting and the 4-bit NLL table, but do not overgeneralize it to standard Transformers."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Hongren Chen","Jiayang He"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["hybrid-language-model","state-quantization","recurrent-state","post-training-quantization","observability"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30950","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.30950","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.31093","title":"Block Sparse Attention with Log-Linear Complexity","abstract":"Scaling language models to long contexts is limited by the quadratic cost of self-attention. Block sparse attention offers an efficient alternative, but selecting the retained blocks remains a bottleneck. Conventional block selection requires scoring all query-block pairs and therefore remains quadratic in sequence length. To address this issue, we propose PISA, a block-sparse attention mechanism that employs a pyramid Top-$K$ selection strategy. The main idea is to gradually narrow down the can","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-25T10:28:58Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_31093.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 block-sparse attention 里一个很具体但关键的瓶颈：虽然稀疏注意力的真正 attention 计算可以做到近线性，但 Top-K block selection 往往还要让每个 query 扫过所有候选块，整体仍接近二次复杂度，长上下文下成本很高。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在长上下文训练与推理效率。它不是在改语言建模目标，也不是在改 Transformer 主体结构，而是在 sparse attention 的路由阶段把复杂度从 O(N^2/C) 压到 O(N log N)。如果这个路由在实际 kernel 上也成立，就意味着 trainable sparse attention 更有机会进入真正的长序列预训练，而不只是停留在理论上 attention 稀疏、实际上 selection 很贵的状态。","technical_approach":"方法叫 PISA。核心思路是把 key blocks 做成一个 coarse-to-fine 的金字塔层级，而不是在最细粒度上一次性对所有块打分。最底层是固定大小的 block；更高层通过相邻 block pooling 得到更粗的表示。路由时先从最粗层开始，只在一个受限候选集上计算 LogSumExp 分数并取 Top-K；被选中的粗块再展开到下一层的子块，继续筛选，直到最细层。这样每个 query 在每一层只看有限个候选，层数是 O(log N)，因此单 query 路由复杂度变成 O(log N)，整段序列是 O(N log N)。\n\n和传统 BSA 相比，区别不在于最终仍做 block-level Top-K，而在于“怎么找到这些块”。传统方法需要全扫描所有候选块；PISA 用层级搜索避免了全扫描。作者还强调了硬件实现：用 Triton 写了 hardware-aware kernel，把多层 routing 和 LSE scoring 融合，避免中间候选反复落盘和读回，也不显式构造稠密 query-key score matrix。这一点很关键，因为很多稀疏方法理论复杂度好看，但 kernel 不友好，最后跑不出来。","experiments":"实验结论比较直接。语言建模任务上，PISA 相比传统 block-sparse attention baseline BSA，常识推理 benchmark 上性能大体可比，检索类任务更好。作者把这个结果解释为：coarse-to-fine 搜索在降低 selection 成本的同时，没有明显丢掉真正相关的远程块。效率方面，Top-K selection benchmark 做到了最长 256K token，结果显示层级路由比单层全扫描选择更高效。\n\n从正文片段能确认的硬指标主要是复杂度：PISA 的 prefill 是 O(N log N)，decode 是 O(log N)；而表 1 中多种 trainable sparse attention 方法仍是 prefill O(N^2)、decode O(N)。当前给出的内容没有完整训练配置、模型规模、具体 benchmark 分数和 wall-clock 数字，因此不宜补写具体提升百分比。泛读时重点看复杂度表、路由算法图和 256K selection benchmark。","reading_guide":"建议先看 Introduction 和 Table 1，确认它到底在优化哪一段成本，以及和 BSA、NSA、MoBA 这些方法的差别。然后看方法部分里金字塔构建、LSE 打分、Top-K 展开这三个步骤，理解为什么复杂度能从全扫描变成对数层级搜索。最后看 kernel 实现和 256K benchmark，判断它是不是只有理论优势，还是在硬件上也站得住。\n\n如果只是判断是否值得跟进，30 到 45 分钟足够。做长上下文训练系统或 sparse attention kernel 的读者，可以再细看 Triton kernel 融合方式和 decode 路径。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific bottleneck in block-sparse attention: even when the actual sparse attention computation is near-linear, Top-K block selection often remains effectively quadratic because each query still scores all candidate blocks.","importance":"Its relevance is mainly on the efficiency side of long-context pretraining and inference. It does not change the language-modeling objective or the core Transformer block. Instead, it reduces the routing cost of trainable sparse attention from exhaustive block scanning to a log-linear procedure. If the claimed kernel efficiency holds in practice, this makes sparse attention more plausible for real long-context training rather than only looking attractive on paper while being dominated by selection overhead.","technical_approach":"$1d","experiments":"The empirical message is straightforward. On language-modeling evaluations, PISA achieves performance comparable to the conventional block-sparse baseline BSA on commonsense reasoning benchmarks, while doing better on retrieval-oriented tasks. The interpretation is that coarse-to-fine routing reduces selection cost without discarding the blocks that matter for long-range access.\n\nOn the efficiency side, the paper reports Top-K selection benchmarks up to 256K tokens, where hierarchical routing is more efficient than conventional single-level selection. deployment side of foundation models rather than pretraining itself. Its useful contribution is to treat two PTQ failure modes jointly: mismatch between the quantization objective and model-level behavior, and outdated first- and second-order signals during sequential quantization. For teams serving large base models, the message is practical: local activation reconstruction is not enough when low-bit quantization starts to perturb logits and downstream behavior. For intermediate Transformer blocks, it minimizes the MSE between quantized and full-precision block outputs; for the final block, it uses KL divergence over the output distribution. Before quantizing each block, it refreshes both gradient and Hessian estimates using backward passes through that block, avoiding the stale-curvature issue in global-objective PTQ methods that compute Fisher or Hessian information only once. For the first-order term, it computes exact gradients instead of relying on the Taylor-style approximation used by FOEM. A trust-region scaling mechanism bounds the gradient compensation step, which is important because first-order corrections can otherwise induce large and unstable weight updates. The paper also derives efficient Hessian approximations for the block-wise objectives and keeps the computational complexity at the same order as vanilla GPTQ, O(max{d_col^3, d_row·d_col^2}).","experiments":"The evaluation covers multiple model families and compares against GPTQ, QuIP, global-objective PTQ variants, and FOEM-like first-order methods. Earlier acoustic content can become relevant much later, so simply dropping old acoustic states is unsafe. The key difficulty is timing: if compression happens only after the full input is finished, peak memory has already been paid; if compression happens too aggressively, the model may lose real-time listening/speaking behavior or long-range understanding. Since the Softmax function is invariant to absolute shifts in logits (depending only on relative values), the authors subtract a scalar multiple of the vocabulary-row mean ($t \\times \\mu$) from every row of the output head before quantization. They search for the scalar $t$ on a validation set to minimize the KL divergence between the quantized and original model's output distributions. A reduction in sycophantic agreement is not necessarily a gain in factual reliability; the model may simply move from incorrect agreement to uncertainty. For post-training and reward design, the result argues for separating correct preservation, correction, calibrated uncertainty, and avoidable hedging instead of using a single anti-sycophancy score.","technical_approach":"The study builds a Chinese factual yes/no evaluation from real search-query-derived questions. It introduces prompts containing incorrect user beliefs and compares model outputs under baseline, belief-only, and anti-sycophancy variants. The main methodological choice is matched transition analysis. For the same question, responses under different prompt conditions are classified as correct, incorrect, or uncertain, and then transitions are counted. This makes it possible to distinguish correct-to-incorrect, correct-to-uncertain, and uncertain-to-incorrect pathways. Incorrect user beliefs induce multiple transition patterns, with substantial variation across models and reasoning modes. Reasoning-enabled generation tends to reduce correct-to-uncertain transitions, but increases uncertain-to-incorrect transitions. Its effect on correct-to-incorrect transitions is model-dependent. model","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-25T14:43:43Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_31342.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是 修复知识过时”这条路径本身就不稳。第二，这个问题和训练信号设计有关：模型显然会过度信任显式上下文，而缺少“何时不该信”的监督。对持续预训练、知识更新、检索增强训练和 verifier 设计，这篇工作都提供了很具体的 failure mode。贡献量级主要体现在 benchmark 与机制分析，而不是提出了新的通用训练算法。","technical_approach":"技术上分三层。第一层是数据构建。作者整理了 317 个经过人工核验的 knowledge reversals，覆盖医学、法律、软件和平台政策四个领域，每个样本都绑定带日期的官方来源，并明确区分历史上有效的说法与当前有效的说法。这个数据集的价值在于它不是一般的“知识冲突”，而是有清晰 validity boundary 的时间反转案例。\n\n第二层是行为评测设计。作者不是简单测“给错文档后模型会不会错”，而是只统计一种更严格的 poisoning：同一个模型在无检索时答对，但加入过时文档后被翻错。这样可以把检索带来的净伤害单独量化出来。实验同时比较 neutral retrieval prompt 和显式要求遵循文档的 prompt，以区分模型是被上下文自然带偏，还是被指令进一步放大。\n\n第三层是 temporal applicability 与内部机制分析。作者在 50 个源可验证的 reversal 上做 matched experiment：保持历史证据文本不变，只改变 evaluation date，看模型是否会在“当年有效”和“现在失效”之间切换信任策略。结果显示，单给日期变化，模型只会 modestly 调整；若把 validity boundary 明确写出来，答案会显著改变。这说明问题不只是缺少日期信息，而是模型不会把日期映射成“是否仍适用”的判断。进一步的 causal intervention 分析表明，关于适用时间的信息可以直接改变最终答案，而匹配控制干预几乎无效；注意力分析则提示，部分组件在早期就参与了这种证据仲裁。\n\n此外，作者还测试了一个固定 hybrid reranker，把语义相关性、文档新近性和 supersession cue 结合起来。它在日期元数据可靠时能稳定降低 poisoning，说明问题的一部分可以在 retrieval 侧缓解，但不能完全替代生成模型自身的 validity-aware arbitration。","experiments":"实验覆盖 12 个模型，包括 6 个 API 模型和 6 个开源模型，参数规模从 4B 到 72B。跨领域行为实验重点看 Qwen2.5 与 Llama-3.1 的大小模型；temporal applicability 实验覆盖 Qwen2.5-7B/72B 和 Llama-3.1-8B/70B；因果分析主要聚焦 72B 与 70B 模型。\n\n最核心的数字有三组。第一，过时检索文档在没有“请遵循文档”指令时，仍能把 Llama 的正确答案翻错 30%，把 Qwen 的正确答案翻错 37%。这说明 poisoning 不是 prompt engineering 才会出现，而是模型默认就会对检索证据过度让步。第二，若显式要求遵循文档，这个翻错率会进一步升到 66% 和 75%，表明 instruction-following 会放大 selective trust failure。第三，matched current evidence 几乎总能被模型采纳，说明模型并非不会用检索，而是不会可靠地区分“该信”和“不该信”。\n\n作者还观察到，近期医学 reversal 比长期已确立的 reversal 更难，说明 temporal drift 越近、知识边界越细，模型越难处理。日期切换实验里，单独改变 evaluation date 只能带来有限行为变化；而显式标注 validity boundary 后，模型答案会明显翻转，这个对比很关键，因为它把“知道日期”与“理解适用性”区分开了。reranker 实验则显示，只要元数据可信，结合 recency 与 supersession cue 的检索排序能持续减轻 poisoning，但效果受元数据质量约束。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但如果你做 RAG、持续预训练或知识更新，建议读得稍细。先看 Introduction 和任务定义，确认作者如何把 stale-document poisoning 与一般错误检索、对抗注入、参数知识冲突区分开。然后重点看 benchmark construction：317 个 reversal 的来源标准、时间边界定义、以及如何验证“旧说法曾经正确”。这决定了实验是否可信。\n\n实验部分优先看三块：一是 poisoning rate 的主表，特别是 neutral prompt 与 follow-document prompt 的差异；二是 fixed-evidence, changed-date 的 temporal applicability 实验；三是 causal intervention 与 reranker 结果。若时间有限，30 到 40 分钟足够抓住主结论。若你关心训练改进方向，值得多看作者对 attention 和内部决策过程的分析，因为这里直接指向“如何让模型学会有条件地信任检索”。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a temporal failure mode in retrieval-augmented generation: a model answers correctly without retrieval, but becomes wrong after seeing a document that used to be valid and is now outdated. The authors call this stale-document poisoning. The key issue is not merely document age. It is temporal applicability: whether the retrieved evidence still governs the answer at the time the question is asked. A reliable model should not just follow context or prefer newer text blindly; it should decide whether the evidence is still applicable.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is directly relevant to foundation-model research. First, it exposes a concrete conflict between parametric knowledge, retrieved evidence, and time conditioning. If a model cannot arbitrate that conflict, then RAG is not a reliable fix for stale model knowledge. Second, the failure points to a training-signal gap: models are taught to use context, but not consistently taught when not to trust it. That matters for continual pretraining, retrieval-aware training, knowledge updating, and verifier design. We introduce Backbone-Adaptive Evidence Routing (BAER), which adapts the evidence mechanism while preserving candidate symmetry: swapping the two responses may reverse the preference but cannot change its strength. BAER separates each expert's signed preference from candidate-invariant reliability and build","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-25T03:26:52Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_30751.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 pairwise LLM judge 的协议选择问题：直接比较、CoT、rubric、校准、参考答案验证等证据机制在不同 benchmark 和 judge backbone 上表现不同，固定一种协议会丢失信息；但自适应选择又容易引入候选顺序偏差。","importance":"它对预训练研究的价值主要在评测侧。当前大模型训练越来越依赖模型裁判来筛选数据、评估开放式回答、构造偏好数据和做迭代训练。如果 judge 协议随 benchmark 或 backbone 失配，偏好数据和评测排名都会变得不稳定。BAER 的贡献是把“自适应证据选择”和“候选交换对称性”同时纳入设计，给 pairwise judge 提供了一个更稳的工程框架。","technical_approach":"BAER 的核心约束是候选对称性：交换两个回答后，偏好方向可以反转，但置信强度不能变化，形式上要求 signed score 满足 s(Tx) = -s(x)。方法先把每个 judging protocol 的输出拆成两部分：signed preference 表示偏向 A 还是 B 以及强度；candidate-invariant reliability 表示这个专家在当前样本上的可靠性，并且不能依赖 A/B 展示顺序。专家库包含 9 类协议，包括双向直接判断、自一致性、CoT、rubric judging、score-then-choose、PRePair、PriDe、CalibraEval，以及一个内部 deliberate A-vs-B expert。每个专家可在两个展示顺序下运行，并把概率映射回规范候选。方法计算专家对候选 a 的平均偏好概率、顺序不稳定性，并形成原始 log-odds 与 reliability-shrunk log-odds；顺序越不稳定，专家权重越被下调。BAER 提供三种 head：evidence stacking 组合多个专家信号；reliability-based routing 根据候选无关可靠性为每个样本选择专家；candidate-blind reference verification 先构造不看候选的参考答案，再用参考答案验证候选。head 的选择在 verification，因为它们对应两类常见问题：专家不稳定和专家共同犯错。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses protocol selection for pairwise LLM judging. Direct comparison, chain-of-thought judging, rubric-based judging, calibration methods, and reference-based verification can each help under different benchmark-backbone conditions. A fixed protocol wastes useful evidence, but adaptive protocol choice can introduce order bias if it depends on which candidate is shown first.","importance":"The work is most relevant as an evaluation and data-quality tool. Modern LLM development increasingly uses model judges for open-ended evaluation, preference-data construction, response filtering, and iterative training. If the judging protocol is mismatched to the benchmark or the judge backbone, both evaluation scores and learned preferences can become unstable. BAER is useful because it treats adaptive evidence selection and candidate-exchange symmetry as joint requirements rather than separate patches.","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"The experiments cover four benchmarks and two 8B judge backbones, yielding eight benchmark-backbone conditions. BAER achieves the highest test accuracy among the compared methods, with gains over the strongest external baseline ranging from +0.87 to +7.32 percentage points. The development analysis clarifies when each head helps: stacking is useful when expert signals are complementary; routing helps when experts disagree and reliability features identify the better expert on a given pair; reference verification helps when pairwise experts tend to share the same error and need an independent evidence source. The condition-level freezing of the selected head is an important part of the protocol, because otherwise adaptation can degenerate into test-time protocol shopping.","reading_guide":"A focused skim takes about 25–35 minutes. An agent may copy or paraphrase a retrieved belief, while admitting a false claim exposes subsequent agents to it. To study this problem, we introduce the Correlated Promotion Benchmark (CPB), which evaluates whether candidate claims should be admitted to shared memory.CPB-Static constructs a frozen test split from publicly annotated sources with fixed gold actions. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-25T04:48:31Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_30813.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"共享 Agent 记忆里的“证据独立性”很难评估。多个 Agent 可能只是复述同一个错误来源，但写入共享 store 后会被后续 Agent 当作新的支持证据。CPB 要测的是：一个候选 claim 是否应该进入共享记忆，以及错误写入之后会怎样传播。","importance":"这不是预训练方法论文，但对长期 Agent、记忆系统和检索增强系统有诊断价值。它把“memory admission”从静态去重问题推进到带反馈的系统问题：写入、检索、复述、再写入会形成闭环。对基座研究的启发在于，外部记忆和多 Agent 协作会改变模型输入分布，也会放大错误证据的相关性；只看单轮准确率或多数投票是不够的。","technical_approach":"方法由两个部分组成。CPB-Static 从公开标注来源构造冻结测试集，并加入作者设计的多 Agent 场景、agent observations、expected reach、severity 和 consumer tasks。gold action 由固定规则给出：只有在 lineage collapse 后仍有足够独立支持的 claim 才应 promote，否则应 withholding。CPB-Live 则运行一个真实的共享 store。每轮中 Agent 读取当前共享记忆，结合分配到的来源产生候选 claim；admission policy 在 promote、request_evidence、keep_private、abstain 之间选择。只有 promote 会改变共享 store。关键设计是 lineage 由场景预先固定，而不是由被测策略根据文本相似度推断。这样可以区分“独立来源支持”和“同源复述”。Live 设置还记录每次 write 和 retrieval，并用一个只依赖 store 的 consumer 回答问题，从日志中读出 exposure，从 consumer answer 中读出 adoption。","experiments":"实验覆盖八类 admission policy 和四个 Agent family，包括 voting、lineage-collapse、confidence、source-type、judge policy，以及来自 Mem0、A-MemGuard 一类系统的组件。主要现象比较稳定：第一，consumer 只看共享记忆时，几乎总会采纳没有竞争真相的错误 belief。第二，严格拒绝 copy 的策略会同时拒掉大量真实 claim，召回损失明显。第三，直接回答型或宽松 judge 往往接近 share-all，会放入大量错误内容。第四，judge filter 只能把损害降低到有限程度，不能彻底解决复述造成的虚假多数。第五，表现相对稳的是基于 source-type 的治理规则，它不是依赖文本相似度，而是利用来源类型字段来限制不可靠写入。论文还分析了 competing truth、rewording、arrival order 对 admission 和后续 adoption 的影响。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20–30 分钟。重点看 Method 中 CPB-Static 与 CPB-Live 的区别，以及 lineage collapse 为什么必须由场景固定，而不能由策略自己推断。实验部分建议关注三类结果：错误 belief 被 consumer 采纳的比例；拒绝 copy 对真实 claim 召回的代价；source-type gate 与 judge/voting/lineage-collapse 的差异。对做通用 Agent memory 的人，Live logging protocol 比具体排行榜更值得看。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies when a claim should be admitted into shared agent memory. The hard case is correlated evidence: later agents may paraphrase a retrieved false belief, and the shared store may then treat repeated copies as independent support.","importance":"The work is mainly an evaluation and diagnostic contribution, not a pretraining paper. Its relevance comes from the feedback loop it exposes in memory-augmented agent systems. Once a false item enters shared memory, it changes future inputs, future writes, and downstream answers. This is a useful warning for long-horizon agent evaluation and retrieval-augmented systems: majority agreement and semantic deduplication can both be misleading when sources are correlated.","technical_approach":"CPB has two modes. CPB-Static builds a frozen benchmark from publicly annotated sources and adds an authored multi-agent envelope, including observations, expected reach, severity, and consumer tasks. A fixed gold rule promotes claims only when enough independent support remains after lineage collapse. CPB-Live runs agents against an actual shared store. Each agent observes assigned sources, retrieves current memory, emits candidate claims, and an admission policy chooses among promote, request_evidence, keep_private, and abstain. Only promote makes the claim visible to later agents. The central design choice is to fix source lineage in the scenario rather than infer it from text similarity. The live system logs every write and retrieval, then probes a consumer that answers from the store alone, allowing exposure and adoption to be measured rather than assumed.","experiments":"The study evaluates eight admission policies across four agent families, including voting, lineage collapse, confidence-based rules, source-type gates, judge-based policies, and components inspired by systems such as Mem0 and A-MemGuard. The reported patterns are consistent. Consumers relying only on shared memory tend to repeat uncontested false beliefs. Policies that aggressively reject copies also reject many true claims. Answer-oriented or permissive judges admit much of what share-all admits. Judge filters reduce damage but do not remove it. The most reliable rule in these experiments is a source-type gate, which limits false adoption while still allowing answers. The paper also studies the effects of competing true evidence, rewording, and arrival order.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. A common hypothesis is that models struggle to integrate multiple components, leading to training interventions to improve compositional binding. Existing benchmarks evaluate captions only in their composed form, making it impossible to separate the cost of joint reasoning from the cost of recognizi","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-25T16:09:42Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_31456.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇文章要解决的是一个长期被笼统描述的问题：VLM 在 compositional reasoning 上表现差，到底是因为“组合绑定”本身难，还是因为随着 caption 里对象、属性、关系数量增加，模型对各个基础技能的识别能力就已经在退化。现有 benchmark 只给 composed caption 的整体结果，看不到 joint reasoning 的纯成本，也看不到不同 primitive type 带来的负载效应。作者因此构建了一个可控分析框架，把 compositional failure 拆成两个可测部分：compositional integration gap，以及 skill load。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要在评测诊断，而不是提出新的 VLM 训练方法。它的重要性在于把“组合性失败”从一个模糊标签拆成可归因的误差来源。对于做多模态预训练、数据合成、架构改造的人，这种拆解很有用，因为它告诉你：如果模型在复杂 caption 上掉点，不能直接归咎于 binding 机制差，可能是对象识别、属性绑定、关系判断在高 primitive load 下各自先崩了，而且 self-load 与 cross-load 的模式不同。这样的结论会影响后续干预方向：是改训练目标、改负样本、改数据课程，还是针对 relation/attribute 单独补强。量级上，这是一个结构清晰、诊断价值较高的 eval 型工作。","technical_approach":"核心方法是 COMPASS。数据构造上，作者从 Visual Genome 的 scene graph 出发，用随机游走抽取最多十步的子图，保证对象、属性、关系三类 primitive 在同一局部场景里可控复用。然后基于这些 primitive 生成不同结构层级、不同复杂度的 caption。这样做的关键好处是：同一组视觉原子可以被组织成 composed caption，也可以被拆成 decomposed counterparts，从而在 matched perturbation 下比较两者表现差异。评测协议采用 retrieval 风格，并构造 hard negatives 来定向测试 object、attribute、relation 三种技能。围绕这个测试床，作者提出两类分析。第一类是 compositional integration gap：把 composed caption 与其 decomposed 版本在相同 primitive 集合和相同扰动下比较，测量仅由“需要联合整合多个 primitive”带来的额外损失。第二类是 skill load：把性能建模为 primitive count 的函数，分别看某项技能随着同类 primitive 增多时的 self-load，以及随着其他 primitive 增多时的 cross-load。这个设计比传统 compositional benchmark 更细，因为它不只问“复杂度高时是否失败”，而是问“失败来自哪种 primitive 负载，以及是否真是 joint reasoning 造成的”。","experiments":"实验规模上，作者在 87K image-caption pairs 上分析 compositional integration，在 274K pairs 上分析 skill load，覆盖 object、attribute、relation 三类技能，并评测了多种 VLM，包括标准对比式编码器、显式做 compositional reasoning 的模型、以及非对比式架构。主结论有三点。第一，compositional integration 确实存在稳定成本，也就是 composed caption 相比 decomposed counterpart 会更难，但这个 gap 只解释了总退化的一部分，不足以解释复杂度上升时接近随机的整体崩塌。第二，不同技能对 primitive count 的敏感性不同，而且不仅受自身 primitive 数量影响，也受其他类型 primitive 的 cross-load 影响。换言之，relation 变多不只伤 relation 判断，也可能拖累 object 或 attribute 相关检索。第三，这些模式在不同模型家族中都存在，但幅度和形状不同，说明某些架构或训练目标可能改善了某一类负载，却未必改善真正的组合整合。虽然摘要没有给出具体百分点，全文的价值在于趋势和归因：作者用受控对照证明“组合性失败”不是单一瓶颈，而是 joint integration cost 与 skill-specific load 共同作用的结果。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看方法图和任务定义。重点是弄清楚 composed vs decomposed 的配对方式，以及 hard negatives 如何分别对应 object、attribute、relation 错误。然后看两组主结果：一组是 compositional integration gap 随复杂度变化的曲线，另一组是 skill load 的 self-load / cross-load 分析。这两部分决定了全文的主要判断。若时间有限，先读数据构造、评测协议、主结果图和讨论，30 分钟左右可以抓住核心。若想把它用于设计新 benchmark 或训练数据，值得细看 caption 生成规则、scene graph 抽样策略、以及不同模型家族在 relation load 下的差异，因为 relation 往往是最容易暴露模型短板的部分。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a question that is often left vague in compositional VLM evaluation: when performance drops on more complex captions, how much of that drop is due to the difficulty of integrating multiple primitives jointly, and how much is due to the model already failing at the underlying skills as object, attribute, and relation load increases. Existing benchmarks evaluate only the composed caption, so they cannot isolate the pure cost of composition from the cost of primitive recognition under load.","importance":"Its contribution is mainly diagnostic. For multimodal pretraining and evaluation, the paper turns “compositional failure” from a broad complaint into a decomposable phenomenon. That matters because different interventions target different bottlenecks. If the dominant issue is integration gap, one might change architecture or training objectives for binding. If the dominant issue is skill load, one might instead change data balance, negative sampling, curriculum, or targeted supervision for relations and attributes. This is practically valuable for building more reliable and human-aligned automated evaluation systems (e.g., Reward Models or LLM Judges), especially in scenarios with scarce human annotation data.","technical_approach":"Introduces the DIAL framework. First, it uses abundant LLM pairwise comparisons to separate judge-specific position effects from latent LLM preferences via a graph-cycle criterion. fea","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-25T10:42:20Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2609_31103.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是一个比“会看深度图”更具体的问题：多模态语言模型即便具备一定空间理解能力，也很难把图像中的对象、对应的度量深度、以及问题里的数值约束稳定地绑定在一起。相对深浅判断不够，真正难的是 object-grounded metric reasoning——先从图像中得到带绝对尺度的深度，再把每个对象的几何证据以不会在语言上下文中丢失数值信息的方式送进语言解码器，最后完成带阈值、区间、排序、组合条件的问答。论文要解决的不是单独的 depth estimation，也不是普通 VQA，而是“模型用自己预测出来的 dense metric depth 等模态里都有类似矛盾：底层表征是连续且结构化的，上层语言推理需要离散或 token-like 接口，而一旦只靠最终答案监督，中间几何量很容易在投影和上下文混合中退化。DepthEvidence 给出的 object-aligned geometry token、context 前后双重几何监督、以及把 dense prediction 与语言 reasoning 放在同一模型里联合训练的做法，对 VLM 预训练和统一建模都有参考意义。贡献量级更接近“把一个重要接口做扎实”，而不是改变基座训练主线。","technical_approach":"$2a","experiments":"模型规模为 4B。实验分三层评估。Level 0 是 dense metric depth prediction，论文报告在九个数据集上的平均 dense δ1 领先于所比较方法，说明几何分支本身不是摆设，而是有竞争力的 metric estimator。Level 1 是 instance-level metric depth estimation，考察对象级测量是否准确。Level 2 是 compositional depth reasoning，重点看模型能否把对象深度、空间关系和数值约束组合起来回答问题。作者还构建了 Depth-VQA benchmark，把任务拆成 object measurement、relative reasoning、metric-constrained reasoning 两条 reasoning track。结果上，DepthEvidence 在总体 reasoning accuracy 上领先，同时 general VQA 能力相对 base model 基本保持稳定，没有因为加入几何分支而明显牺牲通用问答。\n\n从全文给出的实验逻辑看，最重要的不是单个榜单数字，而是几组消融所支持的因果链条：相机条件解码器提升 metric scale 恢复；高分辨率 RGB pathway 改善局部结构和边界；dense-to-language interface 比直接依赖压缩视觉 token 更适合对象级几何绑定；context 前后几何监督能显著提高 token 中 metric information 的可恢复性，并转化为更好的 Level 2 reasoning；ranking loss 对相对深度排序类问题有帮助。论文还强调，Depth-VQA 中很多问题要求同时满足对象筛选和数值区间约束，这类题目能更清楚地区分“有模糊空间常识”与“真的保留了对象级度量信息”。摘要里没有给出完整数字表，但从正文叙述可见，作者把 dense prediction、instance measurement、reasoning answer 三者打通了，而不是只在其中一项上做优化。","reading_guide":"这篇适合精读。先看 Introduction，把作者定义的三层任务 Level 0/1/2 建立起来，否则容易把它误读成普通 depth-aware VLM。然后重点看 Figure 2 和 Method：一是 spatial awareness, it often fails to reliably bind objects, metric depth values, and numerical constraints in the question. Relative depth is not enough. The harder problem is object-grounded metric reasoning: predict absolute depth from the image, attach the right measurements to the right objects, preserve those measurements through language-context interaction, and then answer compositional questions involving thresholds, intervals, ordering, and spatial filters. The contribution is not just better depth estimation or better VQA in isolation, but a mechanism for using the model’s own dense metric predictions as evidence for language generation.","importance":"For pretraining-oriented readers, the main value is not a new general LLM objective but a concrete solution to a recurring multimodal interface problem: how to convert a continuous geometric field into a language-consumable representation without washing out the numeric content. DepthEvidence offers three useful ideas: object-aligned geometry tokens, explicit supervision that preserves recoverable geometry before and after language interaction, and a unified model where dense prediction and language reasoning are trained together. However, this multimodal scaling often degrades the language-side reasoning ability originally encoded in the base LLM. Instead of asking whether a representation works for synthesis in general, it separates linguistic content, speaker identity, and prosody, and measures how much of each attribute can be recovered from the representation alone.","importance":"The work is relevant to speech language models and multimodal pretraining, where HuBERT-style features, clustered tokens, posteriorgrams, and codec tokens are often used as intermediate units. A key message is that “semantic token” is not a guarantee of disentanglement. Speaker leakage and prosody retention depend on the interaction between training objective and information capacity, not merely on whether the representation is supervised or discrete.","technical_approach":"The authors evaluate all representations under a shared conditional generation setup. A generative model is trained to synthesize speech conditioned only on the representation. It receives no speaker ID and no reference audio. This removes a major confound in voice-conversion-style evaluation: if speaker information appears in the generated audio, it must have passed through the representation itself. Linguistic content is measured with differential WER between ASR transcripts of the original and generated audio, restricted to utterances where ASR correctly transcribes the real audio. Speaker identity is measured using the VoicePrivacy speaker verification protocol, with EER as the main metric; 50% EER corresponds to chance-level verification. A gender probe on speaker embeddings is also used to detect coarse identity leakage. Prosody is evaluated by decomposing F0 contour error into mean shift, variance shift, and contour correlation.","experiments":"The comparison spans SSL features, supervised tokens, posteriorgrams, and neural audio codec representations. The main empirical pattern is a split into two regimes: some representations nearly reconstruct the original audio and therefore preserve much more than linguistic content; others remove speaker identity more effectively, usually at the cost of losing some prosodic or acoustic detail. The paper is careful about interpretation: the generative evaluation gives a lower bound on recoverable information, because failure to synthesize an attribute may reflect decoder limitations rather than complete absence of that attribute in the representation. Concrete protocol details include the 50% EER chance level for speaker verification and a nearly gender-balanced evaluation set with 52.9% female utterances.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. latent action 更适合低数据动作覆盖的真实机器人视频。对基座预训练来说，这类方法有助于理解视频预训练、world model 和控制接口之间的连接，但贡献更偏向视觉世界模型数据构造与训练接口，不是通用语言模型预训练方法。","technical_approach":"方法的核心是 action forcing。作者先对视频帧间像素位移做点跟踪，得到大量 motion vectors，然后利用自运动在画面中反复出现的全局一致结构，对这些位移向量做 PCA。最高方差的主成分被解释为数据中自然出现的 egomotion basis，前两个主成分权重作为低维动作输入。这样得到的动作空间是 centered affine coordinate system：零向量表示 no-op，标量大小表示运动强度，符号表示反向运动，不同轴可以组合。相比直接用 dense optical flow 或 raw tracks 做条件，这种表示推理时不需要构造完整运动场；相比 learned latent action，它显式保留了邻近性、幅度、相加和反向等结构。训练侧，作者基于预训练视频 DiT/Wan2.1，采用 conditional flow matching。模型学习从噪声 latent 到干净视频 latent 的速度场，并把动作 a 输入到速度网络中，而不是在生成后对样本做固定变换。这样动作能影响视频生成过程中的场景演化。作者还提出 online latent egomotion critic，把像素空间运动蒸馏成 latent 空间的可微监督，用来约束 action following，同时避免反传穿过视频 decoder 或 tracker。","experiments":"实验使用 FrodoBots 大规模真实城市机器人视频。完整数据约 2000 小时，作者选取 11 个城市构成的 88 小时 coherent subset 来适配 Wan2.1。一个关键观察是原始记录命令因为延迟和执行偏差会破坏训练稳定性，因此被当作无标签视频处理。数据中持续倒车片段只有约 55 分钟，约占总素材 1%，但模型仍学到了 backward control，并能与 steering 组合。论文还指出常规视频指标不适合 action-forced rollout，因为没有逐样本 paired reference。作者提出 reference-free action-forcing evaluation，从可控性、几何一致性和视觉合理性评估世界模型。给出的结果以定性 rollout 和控制响应分析为主，重点证明 PCA 恢复的动作基可解释、带符号且可组合。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 25–35 分钟。优先看 Introduction 中关于 FrodoBots 命令标签失效的分析，这是论文成立的前提。Method 里重点看三处：PCA 如何从 point tracks 恢复 egomotion basis；为什么采用 centered affine action space；动作如何注入 conditional flow matching 的速度场。online latent egomotion critic 值得单独看，因为它是把像素运动监督接入 latent video model 的关键工程设计。实验部分重点看 88 小时子集、55 分钟倒车样本和 action-forcing evaluation。附录 rollout 可以快速判断动作空间是否真的可组合。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses the lack of reliable action supervision for training controllable world models from real-world video. Large amounts of egocentric or robot video exist, but synchronized action labels are often unavailable, delayed, or only weakly correlated with the realized motion. The authors aim to recover a grounded, interpretable, and composable egomotion action space directly from unlabeled video, without manual labels, calibrated platforms, or a learned latent-action model.","importance":"The main relevance to pretraining is in data supervision recovery for video world models. Instead of treating controllability as something to be added after large-scale video generation pretraining, the paper turns unlabeled video into action-conditioned training data. It also makes a useful representation point: real robot egomotion is continuous, signed, scalable, additive, and invertible. Encoding this structure explicitly can be more data-efficient than learning an unstructured latent action vocabulary. The work is closer to multimodal/world-model pretraining infrastructure than to text LLM pretraining, but it is informative for unified models that need controllable latent interfaces.","technical_approach":"$30","experiments":"The experiments use FrodoBots real-world rover video, a noisy and unstructured dataset with pedestrians, occlusions, weather effects, uneven ground, and lens artifacts. The full source contains about 2000 hours, while the model is adapted on an 88-hour coherent subset from 11 cities. A key empirical finding is that recorded commands are affected by network latency and can destabilize training, so the authors discard them and treat the footage as unlabeled. Even though sustained reverse motion accounts for only about 55 minutes, roughly 1% of the footage, the resulting model learns backward control and combines it with steering. The evaluation section argues that standard video metrics are poorly matched to action-forced world models because there is no paired reference rollout. The proposed reference-free evaluation measures controllability, geometric integrity, and plausibility.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim is enough for most readers. Focus first on the Introduction, especially the failure mode of recorded FrodoBots commands. In the method, read the PCA-based egomotion recovery, the centered affine action parameterization, and the way actions condition the flow-matching velocity field. The online latent egomotion critic is the most useful implementation detail if the goal is to connect pixel-level motion evidence with latent video generation. In the experiments, check the 88-hour adaptation setup, the scarce reverse-motion analysis, and the reference-free action-forcing evaluation. The appendix rollouts are useful for judging whether the recovered action basis is actually compositional.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看方法部分：用几何运动基替代动作标注的 setup 干净，适合放进 video pretrain 数据管线做小规模复现。","en":"Read the method section: replacing action labels with geometric motion bases is a clean setup and deserves a small-scale replication in a video-pretraining data pipeline."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Exeter","University of Exeter, Exeter"],"authors":["Ashish Sundar","Tiankuo Hou","Zhong Fan","Chunbo Luo","Xiaoyang Wang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["world-models","video-pretraining","egomotion","pseudo-actions","video-DiT"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30595","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.30595","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.30946","title":"OneWorld: Learning Consistent Physics Across Actions in World Models","abstract":"Action-conditioned video world models aim to predict scene evolution under different actions, a capability that is essential for reliable planning, decision-making, and interaction in dynamic environments. However, futures generated independently from the same initial scene may each appear plausible while implying incompatible physical properties, such as friction or mass. This inconsistency can lead to contradictory predictions across interventions, making it difficult for the model to maintain","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-25T07:59:11Z","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2609_30946.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 action-conditioned world model 里一个常被忽略的问题：同一个初始场景在不同动作干预下生成的多条未来轨迹，单看各自都可能合理，但放在一起却无法由同一组潜在物理属性解释。论文把这个问题定义为 shared-world inconsistency。难点不在于生成某一条看起来像真的视频，而在于让多条 counterfactual rollout 共享同一个隐含世界机制，例如相同的摩擦、质量、刚度，同时又允许不同动作带来不同结果。对 world model 用于规划来说，这个约束很关键，因为规划依赖的是“如果我换个动作，世界会怎样变”，而不是若干彼此独立、互相矛盾的想象结果。","importance":"对基座预训练本身的直接影响有限，它更接近物理世界模型和可干预生成建模；但方法上有两个启发。第一，它把“多分支预测之间的一致性”从隐含假设变成了显式训练目标，这对未来做 any-order generation、counterfactual modeling、甚至多轨迹 reasoning 的生成模型有参考价值。第二，它没有直接强迫不同分支的 latent 或输出相同，而是通过一个共享机制解释器和证据聚合分数来约束“是否能被同一机制解释”，这种弱耦合方式比硬对齐更稳，也更符合不同行动信息量不均衡的现实。若关心 world model、agent planning、或生成模型中的 latent mechanism consistency，可以看；若只关心 LLM 预训练主线，这篇属于泛读。","technical_approach":"方法核心由三部分组成。第一部分是 physical mechanism interpreter。它接收每个 action-outcome branch，也就是初始观测、动作序列和对应未来结果，输出对潜在物理机制 z 的分布，而不是输出一个点估计。这样做是为了表达不同干预对物理参数约束强弱不同：强碰撞分支可能把摩擦范围压得很窄，弱扰动分支则只能给出宽后验。第二部分是 shared-world evidence score。论文不是要求各分支 posterior 完全一致，而是相对初始场景先验 p(z|I0) 来衡量多分支是否仍保留对同一机制的共同支持。这个 prior-corrected 聚合设计的目的，是避免把“都很不确定”误判成“一致”，也避免信息量大的分支被信息量小的分支拖平。第三部分是把这个分数接入生成模型训练和采样。作者先用 same-world group 与 mismatched-world group 训练解释器，让它学会区分来自同一物理世界的多分支与拼接出来的伪组；随后冻结解释器，用 shared-world evidence 作为额外物理兼容信号，一方面用于 flow training，另一方面在 sampling 时做 guidance，推动多条 rollout 朝可共享机制的区域收敛。论文强调它耦合的是物理解释，而不是像素级未来或机制 posterior 的完全重合，因此保留了动作条件下的分支差异。评估上还提出了 shared-world gap：分别对每个分支单独拟合物理参数，再对所有分支联合拟合同一组参数，二者误差差值就是跨干预不一致带来的额外代价。这个指标把“单条预测像不像”与“多条预测能否共同解释”分开了。","experiments":"实验在三个可控物理环境上做：Push Cube、Push Rope、Pour Water，分别覆盖刚体、可变形体和粒子流体。基线是 ACWM-DiT，并沿用 ACWM-Phys 的交互设置。结果上，OneWorld 相比 ACWM-DiT 将 mean shared-world gap 降低了 84.3%，说明跨动作分支的一致性显著改善；同时 mean M-MSE 还提升了约 7.7×10^-4，表明这种一致性约束没有以牺牲单条 rollout 质量为代价。论文的关键说服力不只在主结果，而在评估拆分：它展示了某些基线单分支物理拟合误差并不高，但一旦要求多分支共享同一物理配置，联合拟合误差明显上升，说明问题确实存在。方法设计上，解释器输出分布而非点估计、使用 same-world/mismatched-world 对比训练、以及训练期与采样期都接入 shared-world signal，都是重要消融点；从全文叙述看，这些组件共同作用，单独去掉任一部分都会削弱 shared-world consistency。论文没有把重点放在大规模模型或数据规模扩展，而是放在机制定义、训练信号和评估协议的闭环上。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，先把 shared-world inconsistency 这个 failure mode 建立起来。然后看 Method 里三件事：mechanism interpreter 怎么定义输入输出；shared-world evidence score 为什么要做 prior correction；这个分数如何分别进入训练和采样。评估部分重点看 shared-world gap 的定义与 simulator-based fitting protocol，这决定了论文是否真的测到了“共享物理解释”而不是普通视频误差。若时间有限，抓住 Figure 2、Table 1 和主消融即可，预计 25 到 35 分钟能把核心思想读清。值得深挖的点有两个：一是解释器是否学到了可迁移的物理 latent，二是这种多分支一致性约束能否推广到更开放的 world model，而不依赖可控模拟器做后验评估。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode in action-conditioned world models: multiple futures generated from the same initial scene under different actions may each look plausible in isolation, yet they cannot be explained by one shared set of latent physical properties. The authors call this shared-world inconsistency. The real issue is not single-rollout realism, but counterfactual coherence: if the initial world is the same, action-independent factors such as friction, mass, or material stiffness should remain compatible across all predicted branches.","importance":"This is not a core LLM pretraining paper, but it is methodologically relevant for generative modeling and world-model training. Two ideas stand out. First, it turns cross-branch consistency from an implicit hope into an explicit objective. That is useful for future work on counterfactual generation, multi-trajectory reasoning, and non-AR generative settings where several outputs should share hidden causes. Second, it avoids hard alignment between branches. Instead, it enforces compatibility through a shared latent mechanism and an evidence aggregation score, which is a better fit for interventions with unequal informativeness. This provides dense, token-level supervision to the student. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) eliminates the need for the external teacher. Specifically, a second frozen copy of the student model, now given the ground truth in its context, serves as the teacher. The student model only receives the problem and learns ","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-25T00:42:31Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_30652.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 reasoning post-training 中一个具体但重要的问题：on-policy self-distillation 里的 privileged teacher 如果一直冻结，会逐渐跟不上 student 学到的新推理行为，尤其是自检、回退和修正能力。冻结 teacher 虽然稳定，但它不能吸收 student 在训练中出现的改进，后期反而会给出不合适的 token-level 监督。","importance":"这篇工作对 pretrain 与 posttrain 接口有较强参考价值。它不是简单提出一个新的数学训练 recipe，而是在研究 dense supervision、on-policy trajectory 和 teacher 更新策略之间的关系。结论很明确：对 reasoning 来说，监督信号的“来源是否正确”不够，监督者还要能适应学生分布的变化。这个观察对 RLVR、过程监督、self-training、continual post-training 都有启发。贡献量级主要体现在三个方面：一是指出 frozen privileged teacher 的动态失配；二是提出每轮共同演化的 DCE；三是用 SRCL 约束推理长度，避免只靠更长输出换准确率。","technical_approach":"论文从 OPD 和 OPSD 出发。标准 OPD 让 student 先生成 on-policy trajectory，再让外部 teacher 在每个 prefix 上给 next-token logits，student 用 token-level divergence 去匹配 teacher。OPSD 去掉外部 teacher，改用同一个初始 checkpoint 的拷贝作为 privileged teacher。teacher 的输入包含题目、ground-truth solution 和 student 当前生成的 prefix；student 只看到题目。这样 teacher 能在 gold-conditioned context 下提供更密集的 token-level 指导。\n\n问题在于，早期 OPSD 固定 teacher。作者用固定错误轨迹做 probe，发现即使 teacher 看到了正确解，它在错误答案结尾仍然倾向于输出 EOS，而不是鼓励反思或修正。与此同时，student 在训练中逐渐学会更多 reflection 行为，teacher 却停留在初始分布，二者失配加大。\n\nDCE 的做法是让 teacher 和 student 动态共同演化。每轮更新后，新的 checkpoint 同时作为下一轮 student 的初始化，也复制出一个 detached、gold-conditioned privileged teacher。teacher 在单轮内仍然冻结，保持训练稳定；但轮与轮之间会吸收上一轮 student 学到的修正能力。这个设计比直接在线更新 teacher 更保守，也比完全冻结 teacher 更能跟上分布变化。\n\nSRCL 用来处理另一个副作用：更强的自检和回退可能带来更长输出。它使用同一批 on-policy responses 的 answer-verified shorter rewrites 作为训练目标，只接受答案正确且更短的自我改写。DCE 负责提高修正能力，SRCL 负责把这种能力压缩到更经济的推理轨迹里。","experiments":"实验使用 Qwen3 系列 8B、4B、1.7B 模型，并在四个竞赛级数学推理基准上评测 Average@12。相对 OPSD，DCE 在 8B、4B、1.7B 上分别提升 35.41、37.15、12.98 个百分点。这个提升不是由更长生成单独解释的；matched-budget controls 显示，在相同或接近 token budget 下，DCE 仍然更好。\n\nSRCL 的作用体现在低预算设置下。论文报告在最低测试的 8K evaluation budget 下，DCE+SRCL 达到 35.07% Average@12，平均每个 response 输出 7,500 tokens。相比 OPSD 的 test-time-scaling control，准确率高 6.60 个百分点，同时平均少生成约 692 tokens。\n\nteacher 行为 probe 是全文最关键的证据之一。在 AIME 2024、AIME 2025、AIME 2026 的固定错误 Qwen3-8B 轨迹上，冻结 gold-conditioned teacher 在错误 response endpoint 给 EOS 的平均概率高达 94.5%，对 Wait 等 reflection cue 的概率只有 22.0%。DCE 训练后，privileged teacher 在 endpoint 上的 EOS 概率从 90.4% 降到 41.3%，对 observed reflection tokens 的概率从 32.8% 升到 77.4%。这说明 teacher 不只是变强了，它在错误轨迹上的局部监督方向发生了变化：从“结束”转向“检查和修正”。\n\n消融方面，作者比较了每轮更新 teacher、冻结 teacher、EMA teacher 和周期性更新 teacher。每轮更新表现最好，说明 reasoning self-distillation 中 teacher 的更新频率是有效变量，而不是实现细节。","reading_guide":"建议精读 Introduction 中对 frozen teacher mismatch 的论证，以及固定错误轨迹 probe 的图表。这部分解释了为什么 gold-conditioned teacher 仍然可能给出差的局部监督。方法部分重点看 DCE 的训练循环：每轮如何从更新后的 checkpoint 派生 student 和 detached teacher，以及 teacher 在轮内冻结、轮间更新的稳定性取舍。SRCL 部分要看 accepted shorter rewrites 的筛选条件，它决定了方法是在减少冗余推理，而不是简单惩罚长链条。实验部分重点看三类结果：DCE 对 OPSD Freezing helps stability, but it prevents the teacher from acquiring revision behaviors that the student learns during training, such as self-checking, backtracking, and error correction.","importance":"The work is relevant to the interface between pretraining and post-training because it analyzes the quality and dynamics of dense supervision on on-policy trajectories. only need to locate sparse evidence, they also need to integrate scattered evidence into a compact, query-conditioned state. When retrieval, evidence composition, and answer generation are all left to the final response, irrelevant context and fragmented evidence often interfere with reasoning.","importance":"The work is mainly a long-context post-training and reasoning-format paper, not a core pretraining paper. Its relevance to foundation-model training is the training signal it exposes. Long-context competence may benefit from explicit intermediate representations: source-grounded evidence, a query-conditioned summary, and then the final answer. This suggests a useful data format for long-context mid-training or continual pretraining, where the target is not only final-answer likelihood but also structured evidence organization.","technical_approach":"H2S uses a Highlight-Then-Summarize pipeline. The model first highlights source-addressable evidence from the original context, then writes a question-conditioned summary that integrates the selected fragments, and finally answers using these intermediate artifacts. The original document remains available during generation, so this is not simple truncation or retrieval-only pruning. The highlight stage preserves traceability; the summary stage turns dispersed passages into a reasoning-ready representation. The authors build H2S-Dataset with 6,647 Evidence–Summary–Answer examples from 11 benchmark families: 4,228 for supervised fine-tuning and 2,419 for reinforcement learning. H2S-RL uses a deterministic trajectory-level reward that covers final-answer correctness, evidence grounding, span coverage, summary quality, and output structure. This differs from long-context RL setups that mainly reward the final answer and leave intermediate evidence use implicit.","experiments":"A motivating study on DocQA-RL-1.6K shows a large effect from query-conditioned compression. With Qwen3.5-Flash, direct answering over an average 15.19K-token document gives 72.6 accuracy. A 0.46K-token Claude-generated summary, about 3% of the document length, gives 93.0 when used alone, and 96.7 when combined with the original document. A self-generated 1.04K-token summary improves the same model by 13.3 points. In the main evaluation, H2S-Bench covers seven long-context task types under a shared 128K input and 4K output budget. H2S-7B scores 28.56 on average, and H2S-14B scores 32.60. H2S-14B outperforms the evaluated open-source baselines, including QwenLong-L1-32B by 5.01 points and Qwen3.8-27B by 10.17 points. H2S-RL further improves the 7B and 14B SFT checkpoints by 4.88 and 3.44 points. 距离。它设计了两种生成自训练数据的方法。第一种是改造后的 Verbalized Sampling，让模型显式列出多个可能 approach 及其估计概率，再把每个 approach 作为隐藏指令生成完整答案。第二种是 Groot，让模型构建一个分层决策树，并从不同 root-to-leaf path 中采样。对竞赛编程，树结构可以对应算法选择、数据结构选择、实现策略等层级决策；对故事下一章规划，VS 这类非结构化 approach set 更适合，因为叙事决策相互耦合且不一定有自然层级。与高温 IID 采样相比，这些方法要求模型在生成答案前先承诺不同策略，因此更直接改变解法分布，而不是只扰动 token 层面的表达。","experiments":"实验覆盖两个差异较大的领域：竞赛编程和 Multiple IID samples from an LLM often differ in wording but reuse the same underlying strategy. Self-training then reinforces the strategies the model already prefers, and the marginal value of additional samples decays quickly.","importance":"The work is directly relevant to post-training data construction and indirectly relevant to pretraining data efficiency. It separates strategic coverage from correctness and shows that the composition of solution strategies can matter more than teacher size, sampling budget, or the number of correct samples. For synthetic-data pipelines, the implication is practical: filtering only by quality can collapse the training distribution, while controlling the distribution of approaches may preserve the behaviors needed for RL and test-time scaling.","technical_approach":"$35","experiments":"The evaluation covers competitive programming and Next-Chapter Prediction. For coding, the model is trained on Cobalt and evaluated on Cobalt, LiveCodeBench, and OJBench, with special attention to frontier problems defined as problems solved in at most two out of 64 IID attempts. On these frontier coding problems, Groot- and VS-trained models more than triple the base model’s held-out pass@8, while IID training gives little improvement even with 16 times more samples. For Next-Chapter Prediction, training on IID-sampled high-quality plans does not improve the base model, whereas strategically diverse training significantly increases the probability of producing high-quality plans. The trained models also provide stronger initializations for RL and test-time aggregation. A notable result is that strategically diverse but incorrect responses outperform correct IID responses, and student-generated strategically diverse samples outperform IID samples distilled from a 235B teacher.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30–40 minutes.
